atutāṉ
ellām, tām, tāṉ eṉpaṉa irutiṇikkum potuvāyiṉa. 
ituvari, irutiṇikkum potuvāka varum peyarc coṟkaḷip paṟṟip pārttōm. iṉi, mūviṭap peyarkaḷiyum, mūviṭap potuppeyariyum pārppōm.
mūviṭap peyarkaḷ
yāṉ-nāṉ-yām-nām ivi nāṉkum taṉmi iṭattiṟ kuriya orumi-paṉmip peyarkaḷākum.
nī, nīr, ellīr, nīyir, nīvir ivi aintum muṉṉili iṭattiṟkuriya orumi-paṉmip peyarkaḷākum.
taṉmip peyarum, muṉṉilip peyarum oḻinta, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi eṉpaṉavaṟṟi uḷḷiṭṭa piṟa peyarkaḷ ellām paṭarkki iṭattiṟkuriyaṉa.
mūviṭap potuppeyar
	yām ellām 	- 	taṉmiyiṭam
	nīr ellām 		- 	muṉṉiliyiṭam
	avar ellām 	- 
	avi ellām 	-	paṭarkkiyiṭam
ellām  eṉpatu taṉmi - muṉṉili - paṭākki ākiya mūṉṟu iṭattiṟkup potuppeyarāka vantuḷḷatu.
ituvari pārttavaṟṟiliruntu peyarkaḷ, irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭaṅkaḷil, oṉṟaṉi ēṟpaṉavum, palavaṟṟiṉi ēṟpaṉavumāy vantami teriyavarum.
ilakkaṇa viti: iṭukuṟiyum kāraṇakkuṟippumākiya peyarkaḷ, marapiṉiyum ākkappāṭṭiṉiyum toṭarntu vara, viṉiyālaṇiyum kāraṇakkuṟivāy, eṭṭu vēṟṟumi kaḷum cārtaṟku iṭamāki, irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭattu oṉṟaṉi ēṟpaṉavum, palavaṟṟiṉi ēṟpaṉavumāy varuvaṉa peyarc coṟ kaḷākum.
iṭukuṟi kāraṇa marapō ṭākkam
toṭarntu toḻili kālan tōṟṟā
vēṟṟumik kiṭaṉāyt tiṇipā liṭattoṉ
ṟēṟpavum potuvu māvaṉa peyarē.                (na-nūṟpā 275.)
2. viṉiccol
(1) terinili viṉi
cēṇiyaṉ āṭi neytāṉ
ittoṭaril neytāṉ eṉṉum viṉiccol, neytalākiya toḻil muṟṟup peṟṟatiyum, iṟanta kālattiyum uṇarttukiṟatu. eṉavē. neytāṉ eṉpatu terinili viṉi muṟṟākum. kālam veḷip
paṭiyākat teriya niṟkum viṉi, terinili viṉi eṉappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam varukiṉṟa terinili viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷ ceypavaṉ, karuvi, nilam, ceyal, kālam, ceyappaṭuporuḷ eṉṟa āṟaṉiyum tarum.
(1)	ceypavaṉ	-	(cēṇiyaṉ)
(2)	karuvi 		-	(nūl)
(3)	nilam		-	(vīṭu - iṭam)
(4)	ceyal		-	(neytal)
(5)	kālam		-	(iṟantakālam)
(6)	ceyappaṭuporuḷ	-	(āṭi)
neytāṉ eṉṟa terinili viṉimuṟṟuc collāṉatu, cēṇiyaṉākiya neypavaṉiyum, neytaṟku mutaṟkaruvi tuṇik karuvikaḷākiya nūl, taṟi mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiyum, ney taṟku iṭamākiya vīṭṭiyum, ceyalākiya neytaliyum, iṟa nta kālattiyum, ceyappaṭuporuḷākiya āṭiyiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam varukiṉṟa terinili viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷ, pakutiyāl ceyaliyum, vikutiyāl ceypavaṉiyum, iṭi niliyāl kāttiyum uṇarttum. maṟṟiya mūṉṟiyum ivaṟṟiṉ toṭarpāl uṇaramuṭiyum.
oru cila terinili viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷil ceypavaṉ mutaliya āṟum varāmal, kuṟintu varuvatum uṇṭu. aḵtā vatu, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṟintu varum.
kaṇṇaṉ vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟāṉ
it toṭaril uḷḷa ceṉṟāṉ eṉpatu terinili viṉimuṟṟuc collākum. iṅkē, cellutalākiya toḻilic ceypavaṉ uyartiṇi āṇpālākiya kaṇṇaṉ eṉpatum, cellutaṟkak karuvi kālkaḷ eṉpatum, ceṉṟa iṭam vīṭu eṉpatum terivatuṭaṉ, cellutalākiya toḻi lum, iṟanta kālamum terikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ceyappaṭu poruḷ maṭṭum teriya villi. iṅkac ceyappaṭuporuḷ illi.
eti neytāṉ? eṉṟu viṉāviṉāl, āṭiyi eṉṟu viṭi varukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, āṭi ceyappaṭuporuḷākum. etic ceṉṟāṉ? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāl, ataṟku viṭi illi. eṉavē, ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟa viṉikkuc ceyappaṭuporuḷ illi.
eṉavē, teriniliviṉi ceyappaṭuporuḷ peṟṟum, peṟā malum varum. ceyappaṭuporuḷ peṟṟa viṉi, ceyap paṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi eṉṟum, ceyappaṭuporuḷ peṟāta viṉi, ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
uṇṭāṉ, paṭittōm, kaṇṭēṉ
iviyum, ivi pōlvaṉavumākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi eṉappaṭum.
naṭantāṉ, vantāṉ paṟantatu
iviyum, ivi pōlvaṉavumākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉi eṉappaṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: karuttāvum, karuviyum, iṭamum, ceyalum, kālamum, ceyappaṭuporuḷumākiya aṟuvakip poruḷiyum taruvatu teriniliviṉic collākum.
ceypavaṉ karuvi nilañceyal kālam
ceyporu ḷāṟun taruvatu viṉiyē.            (na-nūṟpā 320)
(2) kuṟippuviṉi
ivaṉ poṉṉaṉ
avaṉ kaṇṇaṉ
iv veṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷil uḷḷa poṉṉaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ eṉpaṉa viṉimuṟṟukkaḷākum. āṉāl, ivi karuttāvi maṭṭum uṇarttit toḻiliyum kālattiyum veḷippaṭi yākak kāṭṭa villi. eṉiṉum, poṉṉi uṭiyavaṉāka iruntāṉ; irukkiṉṟāṉ; iruppāṉ eṉavum kaṇṇi uṭiya vaṉāka iruntāṉ; irukkiṉṟāṉ; iruppāṉ eṉavum poruḷ tarumpoḻutu, kuṟippāl mukkālattiyum kāṭṭum.
iṅṅaṉam, karuttāvi maṭṭum uṇarttik tiḻiliyum, kālattiyum, piṟavaṟṟiyum veḷippaṭi yāka uṇarttāmal, kuṟippāl uṇarttuvaṉa kuṟippuviṉi eṉappaṭum. ak kuṟippu viṉi, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṟa aṟuvakip peyarkaḷiṉ aṭiyākap piṟakkum.
kuḻiyaṉ-poruḷ aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippuviṉi						        (kuḻikātaṇi)
ūraṉ-iṭam aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippuviṉi
ātiriyāṉ-kālam aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippuviṉi
ceṅkaṇṇaṉ-ciṉi aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippuviṉi
kariyaṉ-paṇpu aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippuviṉi
iṉcollaṉ-toḻil aṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippu viṉi.
kuṟippu viṉikaḷil, vikutiyiṉāl ceypavaṉ (karuttā) maṭṭumē viḷaṅkum. ēṉiya kuṟippākavē viḷaṅkum.
ilakkaṇa viti: poruḷ, iṭam , kālam ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṟa aṟuvakip peyarkaḷiṉ aṭiyākat tōṉṟic ceypavaṉ mutalākiya āṟaṉuḷ, ceypavaṉākiya karuttāvi maṭṭum veḷippaṭiyāka viḷakkuvatu kuṟippu viṉiyākum.
poruṇmuta lāṟiṉun tōṟṟimuṉ ṉāṟaṉuḷ
viṉimutaṉ māttiri viḷakkal viṉikkuṟippē.
(na-nūṟpā 321.)
(3) viṉiccoṟkaḷiṉ vakikaḷ
(terinili viṉimuṟṟu)
kaṇṇaki vantāḷ
iṅku, vantāḷ eṉṟa viṉiccol, pakutiyāl toḻiliyum, iṭiniliyāl kālattiyum, vikutiyāl tiṇipāliyum veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, vantāḷ eṉpatu terinili viṉimuṟṟākum.
(terinilip peyareccam)
paṭitta peṇ
iṅkē, paṭitta  eṉṟa viṉiccol, pakutiyāl toḻiliyum, iṭiniliyāl kālattiyum, veḷippaṭi yāka uṇarttu kiṟatu. tiṇipāl uṇarttum vikuti iṉmiyāl paṭitta eṉṟa col muṟṟup peṟavilli. eṉavē, paṭitta eṉpatu eccaviṉi yākum. atu, peṇ eṉṟa peyarc collic cārntu muṟṟup peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē, paṭitta eṉpatu terinilip peyarecca mākum.
(terinili viṉiyeccam)
paṭittut tēṟiṉāḷ
iṅkē, paṭittu eṉṟa viṉiccol, pakutiyāl toḻiliyum, iṭiniliyāl kālattiyum veḷippaṭi yāka uṇarttukiṟatu. tiṇipāl uṇarttum vikuti iṉmiyāl paṭittu eṉṟa col muṟṟup peṟavilli. eṉavē, paṭittu eṉpatu eccaviṉiyākam. atu, tēṟiṉāṉ eṉṟa viṉic collic cārntu muṟṟup peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē, paṭittu eṉpatu terinili viṉiyecca mākum.
iṅṅaṉam, terinili viṉimuṟṟu, terinilip peya reccam, terinili viṉiyeccam eṉat teriyanili viṉi mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.
(kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu)
avaṉ nallavaṉ
iṅku nallavaṉ eṉpatu toḻiliyum kālattiyum veḷippaṭiyāka uṇarttāmal, kuṟippāka uṇarttik tiṇi pāl kāṭṭum vikutiyuṭaṉ muṟṟup peṟṟuḷḷatu. eṉavē, nallavaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippu viṉi muṟṟākum.
(kuṟippup peyareccam)
nalla piyaṉ
iṅku, nalla eṉpatu toḻiliyum kālattiyum kuṟippāka uṇarttip piyaṉ eṉṟa peyaric cārntu muṟṟup peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē, nalla eṉpatu kuṟippup peyareccamākum.
(kuṟippuviṉiyeccam)
mellac ceṉṟāṉ
iṅkē, mella eṉpatu toḻiliyum kālattiyum veḷippaṭiyāka uṇarttāmal kuṟippāka uṇartti, ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟa viṉiccollic cārntu muṟṟup peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē, mella eṉpatu kuṟippu viṉiyecca mākum.
iṅṅaṉam, kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu, kuṟippup peyareccam, kuṟippu viṉiyeccam eṉakkuṟippu viṉi mūṉṟu vakip paṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa terinili kuṟippu eṉṟa viṉic coṟkaḷ, viṉi muṟṟum, peyareccamum, viṉi yeccamum ākit tiṇipāl iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappāy varuvaṉavum, palavaṟṟiṟkup potuvāy varuvaṉavum ākum.
avitām-
muṟṟum peyarviṉi yeccamu māki
oṉṟaṟ kuriyavum potuvu mākum.
(na-nūṟpā 322)
3. iṭic col
paṇṭu kāṭumaṉ - emakku aruḷamaṉ
maṟṟu aṟivām - maṟṟu eṉṉi āḷka
iṅku, maṉ eṉṉum iṭiccol kāṭu eṉṟa peyarc collukkup piṉṉum, aruḷum  eṉṟa viṉic collukkup piṉṉum vantuḷḷatu.
maṟṟu eṉṉum iṭiccol aṟivām eṉṟa viṉic collukku muṉṉum, eṉṉi eṉṟa peyarc collukku muṉṉum vantuḷḷatu.
iṅṅaṉam, taṉittu iyaṅkāmal peyar viṉikaḷic cārntu, avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum akattuṟuppāyum, puṟat tuṟuppāyum oṉṟum palavum iṭicoṟkaḷ varum.
avi, vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ, iṭinilikaḷum vikuti kaḷumākiya viṉi urupukaḷ, cāriyikaḷ, uvama urupukaḷ, tattam poruḷ uṇarttum coṟkaḷ, iciniṟi, acinili, kuṟippāl poruḷ tarum coṟkaḷ eṉa eṭṭuvakiyiṉavāy varum.
ivviṭiccoṟkaḷ, peyarc coṟkaḷumākāmal viṉic coṟkaḷumākāmal, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākāmal iṭi nikaraṉavāy niṟṟalālum, peyar viṉikaḷiṉ iṭamāka naṭattaliṉālum iṭiccoṟkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
(1) vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ: ai-āl-ku-iṉ-atu-kaṇ eṉṟa vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ iṭic coṟkaḷākum.
(2) viṉi urupukaḷ: (iṭinilikaḷ-vikutikaḷ) t-ṭ-ṟ-iṉ pōṉṟa iṭinilikaḷum, aṉ-āṉ-aḷ-āḷ pōṉṟa vikutikaḷum iṭic coṟkaḷākum.
(3) cāriyikaḷ: aṟṟu-attu-aṉ-āṉ-am-iṉ pōṉṟa cāriyikaḷ iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
(4) uvama urupukaḷ: pōla - puriya - oppu - uṟaḻa eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa uvama urupukaḷ iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
(5) tattamporuḷ uṇarttum coṟkaḷ: ē-ō-um-kol-tāṉ-maṉ eṉpaṉa pōlat tattam poruḷ uṇarttum coṟkaḷ iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
(6) iciniṟi: ēe-ōo eṉpaṉa pōlat tamakkeṉpa poruḷiṉṟic ceyyuḷiṉ ōciyi niṟikka varum iciniṟikaḷ iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
(7) acinili: kol - āl- ē- ō- arō eṉpaṉa pōla, vēṟu poruḷiṉṟi acinilip poruḷil varum acinilikaḷ iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
(8) kuṟippāl poruḷ tarum coṟkaḷ: kōveṉa, cōveṉa, kaṭakaṭeṉa, paṭapaṭeṉa eṉpaṉa pōla olikkuṟippup poruḷit tarum coṟkaḷum, tuṇṇeṉa, tiṭukkeṉa eṉpaṉa pōla accak kuṟippup poruḷit tarum coṟkaḷum, poḷ ḷeṉa, porukkeṉa eṉpaṉa pōla virivuk kuṟippup poruḷit tarum coṟkaḷum iṭic coṟkaḷākum.
(1)	ūri aṭintāṉ
ūri (ūr+ai+ai - iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupu.
(2)	 avaṉ uṇṭāṉ
uṇṭāṉ (uṇ+ṭ+āṉ) ṭ-iṭinili, āṉ-vikuti.
(3)	avaṟṟit tantāṉ
avaṟṟi (av+aṟṟu+ai) aṟṟu-cāriyi
(4)	mayil pōla āṭiṉāḷ
pōla-uvama urupu
(5)	avaṉtāṉ coṉṉāṉ
tāṉ-tēṟṟapporuḷ (tattam poruḷ uṇarttum iṭic col)
(6)	ēe ivaḷ orutti
ēe-iciniṟi
(7)	nāṭāmāl ūrāmāl
āl-acinili
(8)	maḻi cōveṉap peytatu
cōveṉa-olikkuṟippu
neñcam tiṭukkeṉa naṭuṅkiyatu
tiṭukkeṉa- accakkuṟippu
porukkeṉa eḻuntāṉ
porukkeṉa - virivukkuṟippu
ivi yāvum eṭṭuvakiyiṉavāy vanta iṭiccoṟ kaḷākum. iṉi, akattuṟuppāyum puṟattuṟuppāyum oṉṟum palavumākap piṉṉum muṉṉum varuvaṉavaṟṟip pārppōm.
kuḻiyaṉ (kuḻi+aṉ)
iṅku, aṉ eṉṟa vikutiyiṭiccol maṭṭum, peyariṉ akattuṟuppāy vantuḷḷatu.
nilattiṉaṉ (nilam+t+t+iṉ+aṉ)
iṅku t-iṉ-aṉ eṉa muṟiyē iṭi niliyum, cāriyiyum, vikutiyumākiya pala iṭiccoṟkaḷ peyariṉ akattuṟuppāy vantuḷḷaṉa.
uṇṇāy (uṇ+āy)
iṅku āy eṉṟa vikutiyiṭiccol maṭṭum, viṉiyiṉ akattuṟuppāy vantuḷḷatu.
naṭantaṉaṉ (naṭa+t(n)+t+aṉ+aṉ)
iṅku, t-aṉ-aṉ eṉa muṟiyē iṭiniliyum, cāriyiyum, vikutiyumākiya pala iṭiccoṟkaḷ viṉiyiṉ akattuṟuppāy vantuḷḷaṉa.
atumaṉ - koṉṉūr
iṅku , maṉ eṉṉum iṭiccollum koṉ  eṉ ṉum iṭiccollum peyarukkup piṉṉum muṉṉum puṟattuṟappāyt taṉittu vantuḷḷaṉa.
atu maṟṟamma - iṉi maṟṟoṉṟu
iṅku, maṟṟu-amma eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum, maṟṟu-oṉṟu eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum peyarukkup piṉṉum muṉṉum puṟattuṟuppāyp palavāka vantuḷḷaṉa.
vantāṉō - aiyō viḻuntāṉ
iṅka, ō-aiyō eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ viṉiyiṉ puṟattuṟappāyt taṉittu vantuḷḷaṉa.
koṉṟāṉ kūkū-cīcī pō
iṅkē, kūkū eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum, cīcī eṉṟa iṭic coṟkaḷum, viṉikkup piṉṉum muṉṉum puṟattuṟuppāyp palavāka vantuḷḷaṉa.
tattam poruḷ tarum iṭiccoṟkaḷ terinili, tēṟṟam, aiyam, muṟṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷil varum.
iṉi, naṉṉūl (421) nūṟpāvuḷ iṉṉaṉa eṉṟataṉāl varum iṭiccoṟkaḷip pārppōm.
avaṉ-ivaṉ-uvaṉ
a-i-u cuṭṭup poruḷit tarukiṉṟa iṭiccoṟ kaḷākum.
etu-ētu-yātu-avaṉā-avaṉō
e-ē-yā-ā-ō viṉāpporuḷit tarukiṉṟa iṭic coṟkaḷākum.
nakkīrar
na-ciṟappup poruḷit  tarukiṉṟa iṭiccollākum.
iṉiccelvāṉ - iṉi emmelli
iṉi eṉpatu kāla iṭaṅkaḷiṉ ellip poruḷit tarum iṭic collākum.
aṟitoṟaṟiyāmi
cēri tōṟitu
toṟu-tōṟu eṉpaṉa iṭappaṉmip poruḷtarum iṭiccoṟkaḷākum.
antō iḻantāṉ
a ā eṉṉa uyaram
antō, eṉṉum col irakkapporuḷiyum, aā eṉṉum col viyappup poruḷiyum tarum iṭiccoṟ kaḷākum.
vāḷā iruntāṉ
cummā iruntāṉ
vāḷā-cummā eṉpaṉa payaṉiṉmip poruḷittarum iṭic coṟkaḷākum. ivi tavira, iṉṉum varuvaṉavum uḷa.
ilakkaṇa viti: ai mutaliya vēṟṟumi urupukaḷum, vikatikaḷum iṭinilikaḷumākiya viṉi urupukaḷum, aṉ-āṉ mutaliya cāriyi urupukaḷum, pōlap puriya mutaliya uvama urupukaḷum, piṟavāṟu tattamakkuriya poruḷi uṇartti varu vaṉavum, vēṟu poruḷiṉṟic ceyyuḷil iciniṟit talē poruḷāka varuvaṉavum, aci niliyē poraḷāka niṟpaṉavum, veḷippaṭiyāṉ varum ivi pōlātu, oli, accam, vivu ivaṟṟik kuṟippāl uṇartti varuvaṉavum eṉa, eṭṭuvaki yiṉi uṭiyavāyt taṉittu naṭattaliṉṟip peyariṉ akattum, viṉiyiṉ akattum, avaṟṟiṉ puṟamākiya piṉṉum muṉṉam ākiya ivviṭaṅkaḷ āṟaṉuḷ ōriṭattu oṉṟēṉum palavēṉum vantu, appeyar viṉikaḷukku akattuṟuppāyum, puṟattuṟup pāyum oṉṟu paṭṭu naṭakkun taṉmiyatu iṭic collākum.
vēṟṟumi viṉicā riyiyop purupukaḷ
tattam poruḷa viciniṟi yacinili
kuṟippeṉeṇ pakutiyiṟ ṟaṉittiya liṉṟip 
peyariṉam viṉiyiṉum piṉmuṉ ṉōriṭat
toṉṟum palavumvat toṉṟuva tiṭiccol.
(na-nūṟpā 420.)
ilakkaṇa viti: teriniliyum, tēṟṟamum, aiyamum, muṟṟum, eṇṇum, ciṟappum, etirmaṟiyum, eccamum, viṉāvum, viḻivum, oḻiyiciyum, pirippum, kaḻivum, ākkamum ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum tattam poruḷa eṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ  poruḷkaḷākum.
terinili tēṟṟa miyamuṟ ṟeṇciṟap
petirmaṟi yeccam viṉāviḻi voḻiyici
pirippuk kaḻivākka miṉṉaṉa viṭipporuḷ.
(na-nūṟpā 421)
4. uriccol
uṟumīṉ varumaḷavum
cālap pēciṉāṉ
iṅku, uṟu eṉṉum uriccol mīṉeṉṉum peyarc collit taḻuvi vantu, periya mīṉ eṉap poruḷ tantu, peyariṉ paṇpi uṇarttukiṟatu. cāla eṉṉum uric col pēciṉāṉ eṉṉum viṉiccollit taḻuvi vantu, mikutiyākap pēciṉāṉ eṉap poruḷ tantu, viṉiyiṉ paṇpi uṇarttukiṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam, peyarc colliyum, viṉiccolliyum taḻuvivantu, palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa paṇpukaḷisayam uṇarttum peyarāki, orukuṇam taḻuviyum, palakuṇam taḻuviyum ceyyuḷukkē uriyaṉavāka varuñcol uriccol eṉappaṭum.
peyarit taḻuvi ataṉ paṇpi uṇarttum  uriccol peyar uriccol eṉṟum, viṉiyit taḻuvi ataṉ paṇpi uṇarttum uriccol viṉi uriccol eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap paṭum. av vuriccoṟkaḷ, oru kuṇam taḻuviya uriccol eṉṟum, pala kuṇam taḻuviya uriccol eṉṟum iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti:iciyum, kuṟippum, paṇpum ākiya palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa paṇpukaḷiyum uṇarttum peyarāki, aṅṅaṉam uṇarttum poḻutu, orucol oru kuṇatti uṇarttu  vaṉavum, palakuṇatti uṇarttuvaṉavumāyp peyar viṉikaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkāmal, ceyyuṭku uriyaṉavākip poruṭka urimi pūṇṭu varuvaṉa uriccol eṉappaṭum.
palvakip paṇpum pakarpeyarāki
orukuṇam palakuṇan taḻuvip peyarviṉi
oravā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa vuriccol.
(na-nūṟpā 442.)
(orukuṇan taḻuviya pala uriccoṟkaḷ)
cālac ciṟantatu
uṟu pukaḻ
tavaccēy nāṭṭār
naṉi varuntiṉi
tuṉi kūr evvam
kaḻi kaṇṇōṭṭam
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷil cāla-uṟu-tava-naṉi-kūr-kaḻi eṉṉum āṟu uriccoṟkaḷum, mikuti eṉṟa oru  kuṇatti uṇarttum uriccoṟkaḷākum. ivi, oru kuṇam taḻuviya pala uriccoṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum.
iṉṉum, col eṉṉum oru kuṇatti uṇarttum pala uriccoṟkaḷum, ōci eṉṉum oru kuṇatti uṇarttum pala uriccoṟkaḷum uḷa.
ilakkaṇa viti: cāla-uṟu-tava-naṉi-kūr-kaḻi eṉṟa iv vuriccoṟkaḷ āṟum, mikutiyākiya oru kuṇatti uṇarttum uriccoṟkaḷākum.
cāla vuṟutava naṉikūr kaḻimikal.
(na-nūṟpā 456.)
(palakuṇan taḻuviya ōr uriccol)
kaṭinakar-kāvali uṭiya nakaram (kāppu-kāval)
kaṭivēl-kūrmiyāṉa vēl (kūrmi)
kaṭimāli-vācaṉi uṭiya māli (maṇam-nāṟṟam)
kaṭimārpaṉ-viḷaṅkum mārpuṭiyavaṉ (viḷakkam)
kaṭipēy-añcattakka pēy	(accam)
kaṭiyaraṇ - ciṟanta araṇ (ciṟappu)
ampukaṭivitum-ampi virivil viṭuvōm (virivu)
kaṭikāṟṟu-mikunta kāṟṟu (mikuti)
kaṭimaṇam-putumaṇam (putumi)
kaṭimuracu-olikkum muracu (ārttal-olittal)
kaṭimatu-nīkkattakka matu (varivu-nīkkutal)
kaṭi viṉi-tirumaṇa vēli ( maṉṟal-tirumaṇam)
kaṭi miḷaku  - karikkiṉṟa miḷaku (karippu-kāram)
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷil kaṭi eṉṉum ōr uriccol kāppu, kūrmi, nāṟṟam, viḷakkam, accam, ciṟappu, virivu, maṉṟal, karippu eṉṟa patiṉmūṉṟu kuṇattiyum uṇartti vanta palakuṇan taḻuviya ōr uriccollākum.
ilakkaṇaviti: kaṭi eṉṉum uriccol kāvalum, kūrmi yum, vācaṉiyum viḷakkamum, accamum, ciṟappum, virivum, mikutiyum, putumiyum, olittalum, nīkkalum, maṇamum, karippum ākiya patiṉmūṉṟu kuṇaṅkaḷilum varum.
kaṭiyeṉ kiḷavi kāppē kūrmi
viriyē viḷakka maccañ ciṟappē
virivē mikuti putumi yārttal
varivē maṉṟal karippi ṉākum.
(na-nūṟpā 457.)
payiṟci viṉākkaḷ
1.	taṉimoḻiyāvatu yātu?
2.	toṭarmoḻiyāvatu yātu?
3.	potu moḻiyāvatu yātu? cāṉṟuṭaṉ viḷakkuka.
5.	mūviṭaṅkaḷukkum potuvāka varum peyar etu?
6.	ākupeyarāvatu yātu?
7.	poruḷāku peyariyum, kuṇavāku peyariyum  viḷakkuka.
8.	poṅkal uṇṭāṉ; kārttiki pūttatu-ivievvev
	ākupeyaruḷ aṭaṅkum?
9.	aṉmoḻit toki eṉṟāl eṉṉa? aḵtu ettaṉi
	vakippaṭum?
10.	viṉittokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit
	toki kkuc cāṉṟu kāṭṭi viḷakkuka.
11.	aṉmoḻittokikkum ākupeyarukkum uḷḷa vēṟu
	pāṭṭik kūṟuka.
12.	potuviṉiyāvatu yātu?
13.	irutiṇip potuviṉikaḷāka varupavi evi?
14.	pāṟpotuviṉikaḷāka varupavi evi?
15.	urupu mayakkam eṉṟāl eṉṉa? viḷakkuka.
16.	iṟantakāla iṭinilikaḷ evi?
17.	nikaḻkāla iṭinilikaḷ evi?
18.	etirkāla iṭinilikaḷ evi?
19.	iṭinilikaḷ tavirak kālaṅkāṭṭum vēṟu uṟuppukkaḷ
	evi?
20.	ṟu-ṟum, ku-kum eṉṟa vikutikaḷ evvek kālaṅkaḷikkāṭṭum.
21.	ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟuvikuti evvek
	kālaṅkaḷik kāṭṭum?
22.	etirmaṟi ākāra vikuti evvek kālaṅkaḷik kāṭṭum?
23.	pukkāṉ, toṭṭāṉ-ivaṟṟil kālaṅkāṭṭum. uṟuppu etu?
24.	ciṉiviṉi, mutalviṉiyik koṇṭu muṭivatic
	cāṉṟuṭaṉ viḷakkuka.
25.	aimpāl mūviṭaṅkaḷukkum potuvāka varum kuṟippu
	viṉimuṟṟukkaḷ evi? cāṉṟuṭaṉ viḷakkuka.
26.	yār, evaṉ-ik kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟukkaḷ evveppāl
	kaḷukkup potuvāka varum? cāṉṟu taruka.
27.	ellāviṭattum varum acic coṟkaḷil eviyēṉum
	patti eṭutteḻuka. avaṟṟuḷ eviyēṉum aintiṟkuc
	cāṉṟu taruka.
28.	peyarc collāvatu yātu?
29.	peyarc coṟkaḷ eṭṭu vēṟṟumi urupukaḷiyum ēṟṟu
	niṟṟaṟku ovvor cāṉṟu taruka.
30.	mūviṭap peyarkaḷ evi?
31.	mūviṭap potuppeyar etu?
32.	terinili viṉiyāvatu yātu? kuṟippu viṉiyāvatu yātu?
33.	viṉiccoṟkaḷ ettaṉi vakippaṭum?
34.	iṭic coṟkaḷ ettaṉi vakiyāka varum.
35.	iṭiccol eṉṟu ēṉ peyar vantatu?
36.	uriccollāvatu yātu?
37.	peyar uriccol, viṉi uriccol-viḷakkuka.
38.	oru kuṇam taḻuviya uric coṟkaḷ evi?
39.	kaṭi eṉṉum uriccol evvek kuṇaṅkaḷi uṇart tum?
	aintiṟkuc cāṉṟu taruka.

3. potu
1. viṉāvaki
ic ceyyuḷukkup poruḷ yātu?
aḵtu aravō kayiṟō?
mēṟkaṇṭa toṭarkaḷ viṉāpporuḷit tarukiṉṟa miyāl viṉāvākkiyaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum. MáÇa®, j« khztid neh¡», ‘ï¢ brŒíS¡F¥ bghUŸ ahJ?  eṉa viṉavum poḻutu, āciriyar ac ceyyuḷiṉ poruḷi aṟintu koṇṭu tāṉ māṇavaṉi viṉavukiṉṟār. eṉavē, ivviṉā aṟiviṉā eṉappaṭum.
khzt‹, j‹ MáÇaiu neh¡», ‘ï¢ brŒíS¡F¥ bghUŸ ahJ? eṉa viṉavumpoḻutu, avaṉ acceyyuḷiṉ poruḷi aṟiyāmiyāl ataṉi aṟintukoḷḷa eṇṇik kēṭkiṉṟaṉātaliṉ, appoḻutu ivviṉā aṟiyāviṉā eṉappaṭum.
‘m~J munth fÆnwh? eṉa oruvar viṉavumpoḻutu, viṉavukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟilum tuṇivu piṟavāmal aiyamuṟṟuk kēṭkiṉ ṟārātaliṉ, ivviṉā aiyaviṉā eṉappaṭum. (aravu-pāmpu)
iṅṅaṉam varukiṉṟa viṉā āṟuvakippaṭum. avi, aṟiviṉā, aṟiyāviṉā, aiyaviṉā, koḷalviṉā, koṭi viṉā, ēvalviṉā eṉappaṭum.

khzt‹, MáÇaiu neh¡» ‘v£L¤bjhif {ešfŸ ahit? eṉa viṉavutal, aṟiyāmiyāl avaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷa eṇṇi viṉavappaṭutaliṉ aṟiyāviṉā eṉappaṭum.

ilakkaṇa viti: aṟitalum, aṟiyāmiyum, aiyuṟalum, koḷḷutalum, koṭuttalum, ēvutalumākiya aṟuvakip poruḷiyum nīkkātu, viṉā vakiyākak koḷvar.
aṟivaṟi yāmi yiyuṟal koḷalkoṭi
ēva ṟarumviṉā vāṟu miḻukkār.
(na-nūṟpā 385)
2. viṭi vaki

‘Ú rd« bfhŸthah? -  koḷḷēṉ
maturikku vaḻi yātu? eṉa viṉavumpoḻutu iḵtu eṉa vaḻiyic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi viṭi kūṟutaliṉ, avviṭi cuṭṭu viṭi eṉappaṭum.
‘Ú ád« bfhŸthah? eṉa viṉavum poḻutu, koḷḷēṉ eṉa maṟuttu viṭi kūṟutaliṉ, avviṭi maṟi viṭi eṉappaṭum.
ivvāṟu varukiṉṟa viṭi eṭṭuvakippaṭum. avi, cuṭṭu viṭi, maṟi viṭi, nērviṭi, ēval viṭi, viṉātal viṭi, uṟṟa turittal viṭi, uṟuvatu kūṟal viṭi, iṉamoḻi viṭi eṉappaṭum.
1. cuṭṭu viṭi:
‘br‹id¡F tÊ ahJ? eṉṟa viṉāviṟku iḵtu eṉṟu vaḻiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭik kūṟutal cuṭṭu viṭi eṉappaṭum.
2. maṟi viṭi:
‘rh¤jh ïJ brŒthah? eṉṟa viṉāviṟkuc ceyyēṉ eṉṟu viṭi kūṟutal maṟiviṭi eṉappaṭum.
3.	nēr viṭi:
‘rh¤jh ïJ brŒthah? eṉṟa viṉāviṟkuc ceyvēṉ eṉṟu viṭi kūṟutal nērviṭi eṉappaṭum.
4. ēval viṭi:
‘rh¤jh c©ghah? eṉṟa viṉāviṟku nī uṇ eṉa viṭi kūṟutal ēval viṭi eṉappaṭum.
5. viṉātal viṭi:
‘rh¤jh go¥ghah? eṉṟa viṉāviṟkup paṭippēṉā eṉa viṉāvetir viṉātalāka viṭi kūṟutal, viṉātal viṭi eṉappaṭum. (iṅku ōkāra iṭiccol viṉāpporuḷil vantuḷḷatu.)
6. uṟṟaturittal viṭi:
‘rh¤jh el¥ghah? eṉṟa viṉāviṟkuk kāl valittatu eṉṟu viṭi kūṟutal uṟṟa turittal viṭi eṉappaṭum. kāl valikkiṟatu eṉa nikaḻkālattāl kūṟiṉum amiyum. (uṟṟa turittal-taṉakku nērntatik kūṟutal.)
7. uṟuvatu kūṟal viṭi:
cāttā iravu viḻippāyā eṉṟa viṉāviṟkuk kaṇ nōkum eṉṟu viṭi kūṟutal uṟuvatu kūṟal viṭi eṉappaṭum. (uṟuvatu kūṟal-taṉakku iṉi nērakkūṭiyatik kūṟutal.)
8. iṉamoḻi viṭi:
‘rh¤jh ngdh csjh? eṉṟa viṉāviṟkup peṉcil uṇṭu eṉṟu viṭi kūṟutal iṉamoḻi viṭi eṉappaṭum.
ivviṭi vakikaḷum cuṭṭu viṭi, maṟi viṭi, nēr viṭi mūṉṟum viṉāviṟku ēṟṟa viṭikaḷākum. ēval viṭi, viṉātal viṭi, uṟṟaturittal viṭi, uṟuvatu kūṟal viṭi, iṉamoḻi viṭi eṉṟa aintum viṭipporuḷik kuṟippāl taruvaṉa
vākum. ivvintuḷ, viṉātalviṭi oṉṟum uṭaṉ pāṭṭup poruḷiyum, ēṉiya nāṉkum etirmaṟip poruḷiyum tarum.
ilakkaṇa viti: cuṭṭum, etirmaṟuttalum, uṭaṉpaṭa lum, ēvutalum, etir viṉāvutalum, uṟṟa turittalum, uṟuvatu kūṟalum, iṉattic collutalum eṉa, viṭi eṭṭu vakip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ iṟutiyāka uḷḷa aintum, avviṭip poruḷit tarutaliṉāl viṭikaḷākavē taḻuvikkoḷvar.
kaṭṭu maṟinē rēval viṉātal
uṟṟa turitta luṟuvatu kūṟal
iṉamoḻi yeṉumeṉ ṇiṟiyu ḷiṟuti
nilaviya vintumap poruṇmiyi ṉērpa.
(na-nūṟpā 386.)
3. aṭimoḻi - poruḷkōḷ
(1) aṭimoḻi
veṇṭāmari - veṇṭiṅkaḷ
veṇṭāmari (veṇmi+tāmari) eṉṟa toṭaril, veṇmi eṉpatu niṟattiyum, tāmari eṉpatu pūviyum kuṟikkum coṟkaḷākum. tāmarip pūvilal,centāmari, veṇ ṭāmari eṉṟa iraṇṭu vaki uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ centāmariyi nīkki veṇṭāmariyik kuṟippataṟku veṇmi eṉṟa paṇpuc col aṭimoḻiyāka vantuḷ ḷatu. ivvāṟu oṟṟaṟku iṉamāṉa piṟavaṟṟi vilakkik kuṟitta poruḷik kāṭṭavarum col aṭimoḻi eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu varum aṭimoḻi iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻi ākum.
veṇṭiṅkaḷ (veṇmi+tiṅkaḷ ) eṉṟa toṭaril, veṇmi eṉṉum paṇpuccol tiṅkaḷukku aṭimoḻi yāka vantuḷḷatu. veṇṭiṅkaḷukku iṉamākak karun tiṅkaḷ illi. eṉavē, veṇmi eṉṟa aṭimoḻi, iṅka, iṉam vilakka varavilli; tiṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭavē vantuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu iṉam vilakka varāmal, oru poruḷiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭa varum aṭimoḻi iṉamillā aṭimoḻi ākum (tiṅkaḷ -cantiraṉ)
eṉavē aṭimoḻi, iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻi, iṉamillā aṭimoḻi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil ākiya aṟuvakip peyarkaḷum iṉamuḷḷa aṭi
moḻikaḷākavum, iṉamillā aṭimoḻikaḷākavum varum.
iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻi
1.	poruḷ - neykkuṭam (pāṟkuṭam-iṉam)
2.	iṭam - vayal nel (malinel-iṉam)
3.	kālam - āṭikkārttiki (tikkārttiki - iṉam)
4.	ciṉi - pūmaram (paḻamaram-iṉam)
5.	kuṇam - centāmari (veṇṭāmari - iṉam)
6.	toḻil - āṭupāmpu (āṭāta pāmpu - iṉam)
neykkuṭam, vayalnel, āṭikkārttiki, pūmaram, cen tāmari, āṭupāmpu eṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷil uḷḷa ney, val, āṭi, pū, cemmi, āṭu (tal) eṉṟa aṟuvakip peyarkaḷum, iṉam vilakka vanta aṭimoḻikaḷākum. eṉavē, ivi iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻikaḷ eṉappaṭum.
iṉamillā aṭimoḻi
1.poruḷ	-	uppaḷam
2.iṭam	-	vaṭavēṅkaṭam
3.kālam	 -	nāḷarumpu		ivaṟṟiṟku 
4.ciṉi	-	 ilimaram		iṉamilli
5.kuṇam	-	 cempōttu
6.toḻil	-	tōytayir
aḷam eṉṟavuṭaṉ uppeṉpatum, vēṅkaṭam eṉṟavuṭaṉ vaṭakku eṉpatum, arumpu eṉṟavuṭaṉ nāḷ eṉpatum, maram eṉṟavuṭaṉ ili eṉpatum, pōttu eṉṟavuṭaṉ cemmi eṉpatum, tayir eṉṟavuṭaṉ tōytal eṉpatum tāmē vantu iyiyum. iv aṭimoḻikaḷ iṉṟiyum, aḷam mutaliyaṉa poruḷāti āṟiyum uṇarttum.
eṉavē, uppillāta aḷamum, vaṭakkillāta vēṅkaṭamum, nāḷillāta arumpum, iliyillāta maramum, cemmi niṟa millāta pōttum, tōytal illāta tayirum illi. ākavē, uppu, vaṭakku, nāḷ, ili, cemmi, tōytal eṉṟa aṟuvakip peyarum iṉam vilakka varavilli; aḷam, vēṅkaṭam, arumpu, maram, pōttu, tayir ākiyavaṟṟic ciṟappikkavē vantuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, ivi iṉamillā amiṭamoḻikaḷ eṉappaṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṟa aṟuvakip peyarkaḷum, oṉṟaṟku iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻikaḷākavum, iṉamillā aṭi moḻikaḷākavum ulaka vaḻakkilum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum varum.
2. poruḷkōḷ
(āṟṟunīr-koṇṭukūṭṭu-
tāppici-moḻimāṟṟu)
ceyyuḷil amintuḷḷa coṟkaḷip poruḷ porutta muṟa eṭuttukkūṭṭi amittup poruḷ koḷḷutal, poruḷ kōḷ eṉappaṭum. avi eṭṭu vakippaṭum.
āṟṟunīrp poruḷkōḷ, moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷ, niralniṟip poruḷkōḷ, pūṭṭuviṟ poruḷkōḷ, tāppic poruḷkōḷ, aḷimaṟi pāppup poruḷkōḷ, koṇṭu kūṭṭup poruḷkōḷ, aṭimaṟi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷ eṉap poruḷkōḷ eṭṭu vakippaṭum.
poruḷkōḷ eṭṭaṉuḷ, āṟṟunīrpporuḷkōḷ, koṇṭukūṭṭup poruḷkōḷ, tāppic poruḷkōḷ, moḻimāṟṟup poruḷkōḷ eṉṟa nāṉkip paṟṟip pārp pōm.
1. āṟṟu nīr poruḷkōḷ
collarum cūṟpacum pāmpiṉ ṟōṟṟampōl
mellavē karuvirun tīṉṟu mēlalār
celvamē pōṟṟali niṟuvit tērntanūṟ
kalvicēr māntari ṉiṟiñcik kāyttavē.
neṟpayirkaḷ, karuvuṟṟa paccip pāmpiṉ tōṟṟampōla metuvākak karuvuṟṟu, piṉpu katirkaḷi īṉṟu, katirkaḷ muṟṟāta poḻutu, celvam peṟṟa kīḻmakkaḷ cerukkit talinimirntu niṟpatip pōlat tamatu katirkaḷākiya taliyi vaṇaṅkāmal nērē niṟutti, katirkaḷ muṟṟiyavuṭaṉ, uṇmip poruḷi ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟa aṟanūlkaḷik kaṟṟa aṟivuṭiya cāṉṟōr kaḷip pōlat tali vaṇaṅki viḷintaṉeṉpatu ip pāṭaliṉ poruḷākum.
ip pāṭṭiṉuḷ, neṟpayirik kuṟikkum col eṉpatu eḻuvāyāka niṟka, ataṉ toḻilākiya karuviruntu, īṉṟu, niṟuvi, iṟiñci eṉṉum viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu koḷḷu māṟu iṭiyē amittuk kāytata eṉṉum payaṉiliyi iṟutiyilē tantu muṭittamiyāl, ipporuḷkōḷ, āṟṟu nīrpporuḷkōḷ eṉappaṭum.
āṟṟunīrp poruḷkōḷ, puṉalyāṟṟup poruḷkōḷ eṉṟum, yāṟṟuvaravup poruḷkōḷ eṉṟum, āṟṟoḻukkup poruḷkōḷ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkavum paṭum. (āṟu-yāṟu.)
āṟṟu nīr ārampattiliruntu iṭiyīṭillāmal toṭarntu cellutalpōlap pāṭṭiṉuḷ eḻuvāy mutalil amiya, iṭiyil viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu koṇṭu muṭiyu māṟu amintu, iṟutiyil payaṉili peṟṟu muṭivatu āṟṟu nīrp poruḷkōḷ eṉappaṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: moḻimāṟṟu mutaliyapōl, coṟ kaḷip piṟaḻntu koṇṭu kūṭṭa vēṇṭātu, āṟṟu nīroḻukkup pōla neṟippaṭṭu, iṭiyaṟātu aṭitōṟum ciṟanta poruḷ aṅkāṅku amintu varuvatu, āṟṟunīrp poruḷkōḷ eṉappaṭum.
iṉi orucārār, ēṉiya aṭikaḷi nōkkātu aṭitōṟum poruḷ muṭintu varuvatu, āṟṟu nīrp poruḷkōḷ eṉpar. ataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu varumāṟu:
alippāṉ piṟituyiri yākkaluṅ kuṟṟam;
  vilippāliṟ koṇṭūṉ micitaluṅ kuṟṟam;
  colaṟpāla vallāta collutaluṅ kuṟṟam;
  kolippāluṅ kuṟṟamē yām.
ippāṭalil uḷḷa ovvor aṭikaḷum taṉittaṉiyē poruḷ muṭintuḷḷaṉa.
2. koṇṭu kūṭṭup poruḷkōḷ
teṅkaṅkāy pōlat tiraṇṭuruṇṭa piṅkūntal
  veṇkōḻi muṭṭi yuṭittaṉṉa māmēṉi
  añcaṉat taṉṉa pacali taṇivāmē
  vaṅkattuc ceṉṟār variṉ.
kappalēṟic ceṉṟa talivaṉ vantāl, mi pōṉṟa kariya kūntaliyuṭiḷa taliviyiṉ aḻakiya uṭampil, tēṅkāy pōlat tiraṇṭu uruṇṭa kōriyiṉ veṇmi yāṉa muṭṭi yi ūṭittu ūṟṟiyatu pōṉṟa pacali niṟam taṉiyum eṉpatu ippāṭaliṉ poruḷākum.
ippāṭalil, vaṅkattuc ceṉṟār variṉ, añcaṉat taṉṉa piṅkūntal māmēṉi teṅkaṅkāy pōlat tiraṇṭu ruṇṭa kōḻi veṇmuṭṭi uṭittaṉṉa pacali taṇivām eṉac coṟkaḷi eṉac coṟkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉum koṇṭukūṭṭip poruḷ koṇṭamiyāl, itu koṇṭu kūṭṭup poruḷ kōḷākum.
 ik koṇṭu kūṭṭup poruḷkōḷukkum, moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭu varumāṟu:
koṇṭukūṭṭup poruḷkōḷil pala aṭikaḷil niṉṟa coṟkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉum koṇṭukūṭṭip poruḷ koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
moḻimāṟṟup poruḷkōḷil, ovvōr aṭiyilum niṉṟa coṟkaḷi, av vaṭikkuḷḷē māṟṟip poruḷ koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ palavaṟṟilum, iyalpākiya āṟṟunīrp poruḷkōḷukku ēlātu amintu kiṭanta coṟkaḷi eṭuttu, ap poruḷukku ēṟṟaviṭattuk kūṭṭip poruḷ koḷvatu koṇṭukūṭṭup poruḷ kōḷākum.
3. tāppicip poruḷkōḷ
uṇṇāmi yuḷḷa tuyirnili ūṉuṇṇa
aṇṇāttal ceyyā taḷaṟu.
it tirukkuṟaḷil , pulāl uṇṇāmiyākiya aṟattilē tāṉ uyir aṭiyum naṟkati amintuḷḷatu; pulāl uṇṭāl, aṅṅaṉam uṇṭavarkaḷi viḻuṅkiya narakam, avarkaḷi umiḻ vataṟku vāyit tiṟavātu eṉṟa poruḷ amintuḷḷatu.
it tirukkuṟaḷil, naṭuvē niṟkum ūṉ eṉṟa col, ūṉ uṇṇāmi yuḷḷa tuyir nili eṉa muṉṉum, ūṉ uṇṇa aṇṇāttal ceyyā taḷaṟu eṉap piṉṉum ceṉṟu poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu.
naṭuvē niṟkum ūcal, āṭṭumpoḻutu muṉṉum piṉṉum ceṉṟu varutal pōla, naṭuvē niṟkum ūṉ eṉṟa col, poruḷ koḷḷum poḻutu muṉṉum piṉṉum ceṉṟu poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, itu tāppicip poruḷ kōḷ eṉappaṭum. (tāmpu= ūcal; tāppu eṉa valittal vikāram peṟṟuḷḷatu. ici-col.)
ilakkaṇa viti: iṭiyil niṟkum col, mutalilum, īṟṟilum ceṉṟu poruḷit taruvatu tāppicip poruḷ kōḷākum.
4. moḻimāṟṟup poruḷkōḷ
curiyāḻa vammi mitappa variyaṉiya
yāṉikku nīttu muyaṟku niliyeṉpa
kāṉaka nāṭaṉ cuṉi
ip pāṭalil āḻa eṉṉum payaṉilik kēṟṟa ammi eṉṉum colli, mitappa eṉṉum payaṉilikku māṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
 mitappa eṉṉum payaṉilik kēṟṟa curi eṉṉum colli, āḻa eṉṉum payaṉilikku māṟṟik kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
nīttu eṉṉum payaṉilik kēṟṟa muyal eṉṉum colli, nili eṉṉum payaṉilikku māṟṟik kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
nili eṉṉum payaṉilik kēṟṟa yāṉi eṉṉum colli, nīttu eṉṉum payaṉilikku māṟṟik kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
iṅṅaṉam māṟṟik kūṟiyapaṭi poruḷ koḷḷa muṭiyātu. eṉavē, ammi aḻa eṉṟum, curi mitappa eṉṟum muyaṟku nīttu eṉṟum yāṉikku nili eṉṟum moḻi māṟṟip poruḷ koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
kāṭukaḷ cūḻntuḷḷa nāṭṭi uṭiya talivaṉatu maliyiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa cuṉiyāṉatu, curikkuṭukki mitakkattakka iyalpi uṭiyatu; ammi āḻat takka iyalpi uṭiyatu; mali pōṉṟa yāṉi kāl ūṉṟi niṟkat takka iyalpi uṭiyatu; muyal nīntak takka iyalpi uṭiyatu eṉpatē poruḷākum.
ilakkaṇa viti: ēṟṟa iraṇṭu payaṉilikaḷukkup poruntum moḻikaḷi, ēlāta payaṉilikaḷukkut taṉittaṉi kūṭṭi ōraṭiyuḷḷē kūṟuvatu, moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷ eṉappaṭum.
4. uvamavurupukaḷ
pavaḷat taṉṉa mēṉi
pavaḷam pōṉṟa (cenniṟa) uṭal eṉpatu it toṭariṉ poruḷākum.
it toṭaril pavaḷam uvami mēṉi uvamēyam, aṉṉa eṉpatu uvama urupu.
ivvāṟu varum uvama urupukaḷ palavuṇṭu. avi pōla, oppa, uṟaḻa, māṉa, kaṭuppa, iyiya, ēyppa, nēra, nikara, aṉṉa, iṉṉa eṉpaṉavum piṟavumākum.
kuyil pōlak kūviṉāḷ
tāmari puriyuṅ kāmar cēvaṭi
tarumaṉi oppap poṟumi yuṭiyāṉ
kūṟṟuvaṉ uṟaḻum āṟṟalāṉ
mi māṉum vaṭivam
maḻi kaṭukkum kiyāṉ
kaṟkaṇṭu iyiyap pēcuvāṉ
kuṉṟi ēykkum uṭukki
tuṭi nēr iṭi
cēl nikarkkum viḻi
vēy aṉṉa tōḷ
tēṉ iṉṉa moḻi
(piṟavum uvamatturupē eṉṟataṉāl, pōl, puri, eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu viṉiyaṭiyāṟ piṟattaṟkuriya maṟṟi viṉiyecca vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, peyarecca vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, poruva, ēṟpa, aṉiya, ikala, etira, civaṇa, maliya mutalāṉaviyum uvamavurupu kaḷāka varum.)
ilakkaṇa viti: pōla, puriya, oppa, uṟaḻa, māṉa, kaṭuppa, iyiya, ēyppa, nēra, nikara eṉṉum ceyaveṉeccam pattum, aṉṉa, iṉṉa eṉṉam peyareccak kuṟippu iraṇṭum, ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum uvamavurupukaḷām.
pōlap puriya voppa vuṟaḻa
māṉak kaṭuppa viyiya vēyppa
nēra nikara vaṉṉa viṉṉa
eṉpaṉavum piṟavu muvamat turupē.
(na-nūṟpā 367.)
payiṟci viṉākkaḷ
1.	viṉā ettaṉi vakippaṭum? vakikkoru cāṉṟu taruka.
2.	viṭi ettaṉi vakippaṭum? vakkoru cāṉṟu kāṭṭi
	viḷakkuka.
3.	iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻi eṉṟāl eṉṉa? iṉamillā
	aṭimoḻi eṉṟāl eṉṉa? viḷakkuka.
4.	mōrppāṉi, paccarici, teṉkumari, puṉceyp payir
	ivaṟṟuḷ iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻikaḷ evi? iṉamillāma
	aṭimoḻikaḷ evi? iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻi 
	kaḷukku iṉam evi?
5.	poruḷkōḷ eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
6.	āṟṟunīrp poruḷkōḷic cāṉṟuṭaṉ viḷakkuka.
7.	koṇṭu kūṭṭup poruḷkōḷi viḷakkuka.
8.	tāppicip poruḷkōḷic cāṉṟu kāṭṭi viḷakkuka.
9.	moḻimāṟṟup poruḷkōḷ eṉṟāl eṉṉa? ōr eṭut
	tukkāṭṭiṉ vāyilāka viḷakkuka.
10.	eviyēṉum aintu uvama urupukaḷik kūṟi, avaṟṟiṟku
	eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷum taruka.

4. puṇarcci
1.pala, cila, pū, teṅku, maram, tēṉ
ivaṟṟiṉ puṇarcci
1.pala, cila eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ puṇarcci
1. pala+pala=palapala		1. cila+cila = cilacila
2. pala+pala=palappala	2. cila+cila=cilaccila
3. pala+pala=paṟpala		3. cila+cila=ciṟcila
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷi nōkkuṅkaḷ.
1. pala eṉṟa collukkumuṉ pala eṉṟa collum, cila eṉṟa collukkumuṉ cila eṉṟa collum vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, avi palapala, cilacila eṉa iyalpākavum puṇarum.
2. pala eṉṟa collukkumuṉ pala eṉṟa collum, cila eṉṟa collukku muṉ cila eṉṟa collum vantu puṇarum poḻutu, avi palappala cilaccila eṉa valleḻuttu mikkum puṇarum.
3. pala eṉṟa collukkumuṉ pala eṉṟa collum, cila eṉṟa collukkumuṉ cila eṉṟa collum vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, nili moḻi la vil uḷḷa akaram keṭa niṉṟa l-ṟ ākat tirintu paṟpala, ciṟcila eṉavum puṇarum.
pala+pala=palpala	 cila+cila=cilcila
pala eṉṟa collukkumuṉ pala eṉṟa collum, cila eṉṟa collukkumuṉ cila eṉṟa collum vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, nili moḻi la vil uḷḷa akaram keṭa niṉṟa l-ṟ ākat tiriyāmal, orōvaḻi, palpala, cilcila eṉavum puṇarum. (piṟa eṉṟa miki vitippaṭi)
pala+kali	=	palakali, palkali
cila+paṭi	=	cilapaṭi,cilpaṭi
pala+nāḷ	=	palanāḷ,paṉṉāḷ
cila+maṇi	=	cilamaṇi, ciṉmaṇi
pala+yāḻ	=	palayāḻ, palyāḻ
cila+vaḷi	=	cilavaḷi, cilvaḷi
pala+aṇi	=	palavaṇi, pallaṇi
cila+aṇi	=	cilavāyam, cillāyam
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷi nōkkuṅkaḷ.
pala, cila eṉṟa coṟkaḷukkumuṉ piṟa peyarkaḷākiya valliṉamum, melliṉamum, iṭiyiṉamum uyireḻuttukkaḷum vantu puṇarum poḻutu, avi iyalpāyum, la vil uḷḷa akaram keṭṭu vikaṟpa māyum puṇarum. (vikaṟpamāvatu, akaram niṉṟum, keṭṭum puṇarvatu)
pala+paṭi = paṟpaṭi
cila+paṭi = ciṟpaṭi
iṅṅaṉam, pala,cila eṉpavi piṟapeyarkaḷ vantu puṇarum poḻutu, mēṟkūṟiya vitippaṭi akaram vikaṟpamāyp puṇarāmal, nilimoḻi la vil uḷḷa akaram nīṅka niṉṟa l-ṟ ākat tirintu puṇartalum orōvaḻi uṇṭu. (piṟa eṉṟa miki vitippaṭi)
ilakkaṇa viti: pala cila eṉṉum ivviru collum tam muṉṉart tām varumāyiṉ iyalpākavum, valliṉam mikuntum, nili moḻi īṟṟil uḷḷa akaram keṭa niṉṟa l-ṟ ākattirintum puṇarum. ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ piṟapeyarkaḷ vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, īṟṟil uḷḷa akaram keṭṭum, keṭāmalum vikaṟpamāyp puṇarum.
palacila veṉumivi tammuṉ ṟāmvariṉ
iyalpu mikalum akara mēka
lakaram ṟakara mākalum piṟavariṉ
akaram vikaṟpa mākalu muḷapiṟa.
(na-nūṟpā 170.)
2. pū eṉṉum colliṉ puṇarcci
pū+koṭi=pūṅkoṭi	pū+taṭam=pūntaṭam
pū+cōli=pūṅcōli	pū+paṇi=pūmpaṇi
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷuḷ pū eṉṉum colliṉ muṉ koṭi, cōli, taṭam, paṇi eṉṟa coṟkaḷ vantu puṇarum poḻutu, varumoḻiyil uḷḷa k, c, t, p eṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷukku iṉamākiya ṅ, ṅ, n, m eṉṟa melleḻuttuk kaḷ iṭiyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
eṉavē, pū eṉṉum peyarkkumuṉ varumoḻiyil ka, ca, ta, pa eṉṟa valliṉam vantu puṇarum poḻutu, varukiṉṟa valliṉa meykku iṉamākiya melliṉa meyyeḻuttu iṭiyil vantu tōṉṟum.
iṅṅaṉam puṇarvatōṭallāmal, iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum niṉṟa uyirmuṉ ka, ca, ta, pa mikum eṉṟa potu vitippaṭi,
pū + koṭi = pūkkoṭi
pū + cōli = pūccōli
pū + toṭṭi = pūttoṭṭi
pū + poḻil = pūppoḻil
eṉa, varum valleḻuttē mikkup puṇartalum uṇṭu.
ilakkaṇa viti: pū eṉṉum pala poruṭperc colliṉ muṉ ka, ca, ta, pa eṉṟa valliṉam variṉ, potu vitippaṭi mikkup puṇartalē aṉṟi, varukiṉṟa valliṉattiṟku iṉamākiya melliṉam iṭiyē tōṉṟap puṇartalum uṇṭu.
pūppeyar muṉṉiṉa meṉmiyun tōṉṟum.
(na-nūṟpā 200.)
3. teṅku eṉṟa colliṉ puṇarcci
teṅku + kāy = tēṅkāy
teṅku eṉṟa peyarc colliṉ muṉ kāy eṉṟa col vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, nilimoḻiyākiya teṅku eṉpatil uḷḷa mutal eḻuttākiya te eṉpatu tē eṉa nīṇṭu, īṟṟil uḷḷa ku eṉṟa uyirmey keṭṭut tēṅkāy eṉap puṇarntami aṟika.
keṭṭē puṇarum eṉṟu kūṟāmiyāl teṅku + kāy = teṅkaṅkāy (teṅku+am+kāy) eṉa am cāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalum uṇṭu.
ilakkaṇa viti: kāy eṉṉum col vantu puṇarum poḻutu, teṅku eṉṉum col mutal nīṇṭu, īṟṟuyirmey keṭṭup puṇarum.
teṅku nīṇ ṭīṟṟuyir meykeṭuṅ kāyvariṉ.
(na.nūṟpā 187.)
4. marap peyarc coṟkaḷiṉ puṇarcci
mā+pū		= 	māmpū
viḷa+kāy	=	viḷaṅkāy
kaḷa + kaṉi	=	kaḷaṅkaṉi
mā, viḷa, kaḷa eṉpaṉa marattik kuṟikkum peyarc coṟkaḷākum. avaṟṟiṉ muṉ pū, kāy, kaṉi eṉṟa valleḻutti mutalāka uṭiya coṟkaḷ varumpoḻutu, aṅṅaṉam varukiṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷukku iṉamāṉa melleḻuttukkaḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil varum.
iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum niṉṟa uyirmuṉ ka, ca, ta, pa mikum eṉṟa potu vitippaṭi,
vāḻi+kāy=vāḻikkāy
palā+kāy= palākkāy
eṉpaṉapōlac cila iṭaṅkaḷil valliṉam mikkup puṇarum.
eṉavē, marappeyarkaḷiṉ puṇarcci amippik koṇṭu, valliṉam mikkup puṇarvati aṟintu koḷka.
ilakkaṇa viti: cila marap peyarc coṟkaḷukkumuṉ, potu vitippaṭi valleḻuttu mikāmal avaṟṟiṉ iṉamāṉa melleḻuttu vēṟṟumiyil varappeṟuvaṉavum uḷa. (potu vitippaṭi valliṉam mikkup puṇartalum uṇṭu.)
marappeyar muṉṉa riṉamel leḻuttu
varappeṟu ṉavumuḷa vēṟṟumi vaḻiyē.
(na-nūṟpā. 166.)
5. tēṉ eṉṉum colliṉ puṇarcci
(alvaḻi)
tēṉ+kaṭitu=tēṉkaṭitu
tēṉ+ñāṉṟatu=tēṉñāṉṟatu
tēṉ+yātu=tēṉyātu
tēṉ+moḻi= tēṉmoḻi, tēmoḻi
tēṉ+kuḻampu=tēṉkuḻampu, tēkkuḻampu tēṅkuḻampu
(vēṟṟumi)
tēṉ+kaṭumi=tēṉkaṭumi
tēṉ+malivu=tēṉmalivu
tēṉ+yāppu=tēṉyāppu
tēṉ+malar= tēṉmalar, tēmalar
tēṉ+kuṭam=tēṉkuṭam, tēkkuṭam, tēṅkuṭam
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷil alvaḻi, vēṟṟumi ākiya iruvakip puṇarccikaḷilum, tēṉ eṉṟa colliṉmuṉ mūviṉa meykaḷum varap puṇarntuḷḷami teriyavarum.
aṅṅaṉam puṇarumpoḻutu.
tēṉkaṭitu, tēṉñāṉṟatu, tēṉyātu eṉa alvaḻi yilum, tēṉ kaṭumi, tēṉmalivu, tēṉ yāppu eṉa vēṟṟumi yilum mūviṉamum vara iyalpākap puṇarum.
orōvaḻi, melliṉam vantu puṇarum poḻutu, tēṉ moḻi eṉa alvaḻiyil iyalpātalē aṉṟi, īṟṟu ṉ keṭṭut tēmoḻi eṉavum puṇarum.
vēṟṟumiyil, tēṉ malar eṉa iyalpākap puṇartalē aṉṟi, īṉṟu ṉ keṭṭut tēmalar eṉavum puṇarum.
orōvaḻi, valliṉam vantu puṇarum poḻutu, tēṉkuḻampu eṉa alvaḻiyil iyalpātalē aṉṟi, īṟṟu ṉ keṭṭu varumoḻi valliṉam mikap peṟṟut tēkkuḻampu eṉavum, av valliṉattiṟku iṉamāṉa melliṉam mikap peṟṟut tēṅkuḻampu eṉavum puṇarum.
vēṟṟumiyil, valliṉam vantu puṇarum poḻutu, tēṉ kaṭam eṉa iyalpātalē aṉṟi, īṟṟu ṉ keṭṭu varumoḻi valliṉam mikap peṟṟut tēkkuṭam eṉavum, av valliṉattiṟku iṉamāṉa melliṉam mikap peṟṟut tēṅkuṭam eṉavum puṇarum.
iṭiyiṉam vantu puṇarumpoḻutu, tēṉyātu eṉa alvaḻiyilum, tēṉyāppu eṉa vēṟṟumiyilum iyalpākavē puṇaruma.
ilakkaṇa viti: tēṉ eṉṉum colliṉ muṉ mūviṉa meykaḷum vantu puṇarumpoḻutu alvaḻi vēṟṟumi ākiya iruvarikaḷilum iṟuti ṉakaram iyalpātalum, melliṉam variṉ iṟuti ṉakaram iyalpātalē aṉṟik keṭutalum, valliṉam variṉ iṟuti ṉakaram iyalpātalē aṉṟik keṭṭu, vanta valliṉamātal ataṟkiṉamātal mikutalum ām.
tēṉmoḻi meyvari ṉiyalpu meṉmi
mēvi ṉiṟuti yaḻivum valivariṉ
īṟupōy valimeli mikalumā miruvaḻi
(na-nūṟpā 214.)
2. ceyyuḷ vikāram
toṭṭaṉit tūṟu maṇaṟkēṇi māntarkkuk
kaṟṟaṇit tūṟu maṟivu.
it tirukkuḷaṟil toṭṭatu, kaṟṟatu eṉa niṟkavēṇṭiya coṟkaḷ, toṭṭu, kaṟṟu eṉat tokuttal vikāram peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ceyyuḷil aṭi, toṭi mutalāṉavikaḷi nōkkic coṟkaḷ pala vikāraṅkaḷip peṟṟu varum. aṅṅaṉam varuvatu ceyyuḷ vikāram eṉappaṭum.
ceyyuḷ vikāram, valittal, melittal, nīṭṭal, kuṟukkal, virittal, tokuttal eṉṟa āṟuṭaṉ, mutaṟkuṟi, iṭikkuṟi, kaṭikkuṟi eṉṟa mūṉṟum cērttu oṉpatu vakippaṭum. avi varumāṟu:
1. valittal vikāram
kuṟuttāṭ pūtañ cumanta
kuṟumi+tāḷ=kuṟuntāḷ eṉap puṇara vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam puṇarātu n eṉṟa melleḻuttu t eṉṟa valleḻuttāka vikārap paṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, valittal vikāram eṉappaṭum.
2. melittal vikāram
taṇṭiyi ṉiṉakkiḷi kaṭivōḷ
it toṭaril taṭṭi eṉaṟpālatu, taṇṭi eṉa ṭ eṉṟa valleḻuttu ṇ eṉṟa melleḻuttāka vikārappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu melittal vikāramākum. (taṭṭi-kiḷiyōṭṭum karuvi).
3. nīṭṭal vikāram
īcaṉ enti iṇiyaṭi nīḻalē
it toṭaril niḻalē eṉaṟpālatu nīḻalē eṉak kuṟil, neṭilāka nīṇṭu vikārappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu nīṭṭal vikāramākum.
4. kuṟukkal vikāram
tiruttār naṉṟeṉṟēṉ ṟiyēṉ
it toṭaril tīyēṉ eṉaṟpālatu tiyēṉ eṉa neṭil, kuṟilāka vikārappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu kuṟukkal vikāramākum.
5. virittal vikāram
vetiri ṉelviḷi yummē
it toṭaril viḷiyumē eṉaṟpālatu viḷiyummē eṉa, iṭiyē m eṉṟa meyyeḻuttu virintu vikārappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu virittal vikāramākum.
6. tokuttal vikāram
ciṟiyili vetiriṉel
it toṭaril ciṟiyavili eṉaṟpālavatu ciṟiyili eṉa, yakara uyirmey tokuttal vikārappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu tokuttal vikāramākum.
7. mutaṟkuṟi
mariyitaḻ puriyum añceñ cīṟaṭi
it toṭaril tāmari eṉaṟpālatu mari eṉa mutal eḻuttuk kuṟintu niṟkiṟatu. eṉavē, itu mutaṟkuṟi yākum.
8. iṭikkuṟi
ōti mutupōttu
it toṭaril onti eṉaṟpālatu ōti eṉa iṭiyil uḷḷa nakaramey kuṟintu niṟkiṟatu. eṉavē, iḵtu iṭikkuṟi yākum.
9. kaṭikkuṟi
nīluṇ ṭukiliki kaṭuppa
it toṭaril nīlam eṉappālatu nīl eṉak kaṭik kuṟintuḷḷatu. eṉavē, itu kaṭikkuṟi yākum.
ivi tavira, uriyiṟkōṭal eṉṟa vakiyil, vitiyiṉṟi varukiṉṟa tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal, nīḷal, eḻuttu nilimāṟutal, colnilimāṟutal mutalāyiṉavum ceyyuḷ vikāramākak koḷḷappaṭum.
avaṟṟuḷ cila varumāṟu:
yātu - yāvatu eṉa varuvatu tōṉṟal vikāramākum.
kaṇṇakal parappu - kaṇṇakaṉ parappu eṉa varuvatu tirital vikāramākam.
yāṉi - āṇi eṉa varuvatu nīḷal vikāramākum.
peyar - pēr eṉa varuvatu nīḷal vikāramākum.
taci -cati eṉa varuvatu eḻuttu nilimāṟutal vikāramākum.
ivvāy - vāyil eṉa varuvatu col nilimāṟutal vikāramākam.
ilakkaṇa viti: melloṟṟi valloṟṟākkalum, valloṟṟi melloṟṟākkalum, kuṟṟaṟeḻutti neṭṭeḻut kkalum, neṭṭeḻuttik kuṟṟeḻuttākka lum, illāta eḻutti varuvittalum, uḷḷa eḻutti nīkkalum, ceyyuḷiṭattu aṭi, toṭi mutalāṉavikaḷi nōkki amikka vēṇṭu miṭattu varuvaṉavum ceyyuḷ vikāramākum.
ilakkaṇa viti: aṭi, toṭi mutalāṉavikaḷi nōkki, oru moḻi mutal, iṭi, kaṭi eṉa mūṉṟiṭattum kuṟintu varutalum ceyyuḷ vikāramākam.
valittaṉ melitta ṉīṭṭal kuṟukkal
viritta ṟokuttalum varuñceyyuḷ vēṇṭuḻi. (na-nūṟpā 155.)
oru moḻi mūvaḻik kuṟitalu maṉittē.        (na-nūṟpā 156.)
3.ṇakara, ṉakara-yakara,rakara,ḻakara-lakara,ḷakara
īṟṟuppuṇarcci
(1) ṇakara, ṉakara īṟṟuppuṇarcci
(vēṟṟumi)
maṇ+kalam=maṭkalam	poṉ+kalam=poṟkalam
maṇ+kuṭam= maṭkuṭam	poṉ+kuṭam=poṟkuṭam
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē valliṉamvariṉ, ṇakara ṉakara īṟukaḷ muṟiyē ṭakaramākavum, ṟakaramākavum tiriyum.
maṇ+ñāṟci = maṇññāṟci        	=poṉ+ñāṟci
				=poṉñāṟci
maṇ+māṭci=maṇmāṭci poṉ+māṭci =poṉmāṭci
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē melliṉam variṉ, ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
maṇ+vaṉmi=maṇvaṉmi
poṉ+vaṉmi = poṉvaṉmi
maṇ+yāppu = maṇyāppu
poṉ+yāppu = poṉyāppu
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē iṭiyiṉamvariṉ, ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
(alvaḻi)
maṇ+ciṟitu=maṇciṟitu
poṉ+ciṟitu = poṉciṟitu
maṇ+peritu=maṇperitu
poṉ+peritu = poṉperitu
ivvāṟu, alvaḻiyilē, valliṉam variṉ, ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
maṇ+ñāṉṟatu=maṇñāṉṟatu
poṉ+ñāṉṟatu = poṉñāṉṟatu
maṇ+māṇṭatu=maṇmāṇyātu
poṉ+māṇṭatu = poṉmāṇṭatu
ivvāṟu, alvaḻiyilē, valliṉam variṉ, ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
maṇ+yātu=maṇyātu	poṉ+yātu=poṉyātu
maṇ+valitu=maṇvalitu	poṉ+valitu=poṉvalitu
ivvāṟu, alvaḻiyilē, iṭiyiṉam variṉ, ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
(kaṇ+poṟi-kaṭpoṟi eṉap paṇput tokiyilum, poṉ+cuṇaṅku=poṟcuṇaṅku eṉa uvamittokiyilum alvaḻiyil, orōvaḻi ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ tirital uṇṭu. maṇ+cumantāṉ=maṇ cumantāṉ, poṉ+cumantāṉ =poṉ cumantāṉ eṉa vēṟṟumiyilē, varumoḻi viṉi yāyiṉ, īṟukaḷ tiriyāmal orōvaḻi iyalpātal uṇṭu.)
ilakkaṇa viti:ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilē varumoḻi mutalil valliṉam variṉ, ṇ-ṭ ākavum, ṉ-ṟ ākavum tiriyum; melliṉamum iṭiyiṉamum vantu puṇaril iyalpākum. alvaripa puṇarcciyilē mūviṉa meykaḷ variṉum iyalpākum.
ṇaṉaval liṉamvaraṭ ṭaṟavum piṟavariṉ
iyalpu mākum vēṟṟumik kalvaḻik
kaṉittumey variṉu miyalpā kummē
(na-nūṟpā 209.)
kuṟilaṇivillā ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ
(alvaḻi)
tūṇ+naṉṟu=tūṇaṉṟu	pacumaṇ+naṉṟu=pacumaṇaṉṟu
aracaṉ+nallaṉ=aracaṉallaṉ
cempoṉ+naṉṟu = cempoṉaṉṟu
(vēṟṟumi)
tūṇ+naṉmi=tūṇaṉmi
pacumaṇ + naṉṟu = pacumaṇaṉmi
aracaṉ+naṉmi=aracaṉaṉmi
cempoṉ+naṉmi = cempoṉaṉmi
ivvāṟu, alvaḻiyilum, vēṟṟumiyilum taṉikkuṟilic cārāta ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷiṉ muṉ varukiṉṟa nakaram, muṟiyē ṇakaramākavum, ṉakaramākavum tiriyum poḻutu, nilimoḻi ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ keṭṭup puṇarum.
ilakkaṇa viti: kuṟil oṉṟaṉiyum cārātu, orumoḻi toṭar moḻikaḷic cārnta ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ, varumoḻikku mutalāka vanta nakaram mayakkaviti iṉmiyiṉālē tirintaviṭattut tām keṭutalip poruntum.
kuṟilaṇi villā ṇaṉakkaḷ vanta
nakaran tirintuḻi naṇṇuṅ kēṭē.
(na-nūṟpā 210.)
2. yakara, rakara, ḻakara īṟṟuppuṇarcci
(alvaḻi)
(eḻuvāyttoṭar-iyalpu)
vēy+kuṟitu=vēykuṟitu
vēr+ciṟitu=vērciṟitu
yāḻ+peritu= yāḻperitu
ivvāṟu, alvaḻi eḻuvāyt toṭaril yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka,ca,ta,pakkaḷ iyalpākum.
(ummittoki-iyalpu)
pēy+pūtam=pēypūtam
nīr+kāṉal=nīrkkāṉal
ikaḻ+pukaḻ=ikaḻpukaḻ
ivvāṟu, alvaḻi ummit tokiyil yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ iyalpākum.
(viṉittoki-iyalpu)
cey+kaṭaṉ=ceykaṭaṉ
tēr+poruḷ=tērporuḷ
vīḻ+puṉal=vīḻpuṉal
ivvāṟu, alvaḻi viṉittokiyil yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ mūṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ iyalpākum.
(paṇputtoki - mikutal)
mey+kīrtti = meykkīrtti
kār+paruvam=kārpparuvam
yāḻ+karuvi=yāḻkkaruvi
ivvāṟu, alvaḻip paṇputtokiyil yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ mikum.
(uvamittoki - mikutal)
vēy+tōḷ=vēyttērḷ
kār+kuḻal=kārkkuḻal
kāḻ+paṭivam=kāḻppaṭivam
ivvāṟu, alvaḻip uvamittokiyil yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ mikum.
(yakaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam-mikutal)
pōy+koṇṭāṉ-pōyk koṇṭāṉ
ivvāṟu, yakaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṉ muṉvarum valliṉam mikum.
(rakaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam-iyalpu)
uṇṇiyar+pōvāṉ=uṇṇiyarpōvāṉ
ivvāṟu, rakavīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṉmuṉ varum valli ṉam iyalpākum.
(vēṟṟumiyil valimikal)
nāy+kāl=nāykkāl
tēr+tali=tērttali
yāḻ+kōṭu= yāḻkkōṭu
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ mikum.
(vēṟṟumiyil iṉattōṭu uṟaḻtal)
vēy+kuḻal=vēykkuḻal, vēyṅkuḻal
ār+kōṭu=ārkkōṭu, ārṅkōṭu
pāḻ+tūṟuṣa=pāḻttūṟu, pāḻntūṟu
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ orukālmikkum, orukāl iṉamelliṉat tōṭu uṟaḻntum varum.
naṉṉūl nūṟpāvuḷ (224) mēl eṉṟa miki vitippaṭi, vēṟṟumiyil iyalpātalum, alvaḻiyil uṟaḻlum, taṉik kuṟilic cārāta yakarattiṉ muṉ yakaram variṉ, alvaḻi vēṟṟumi iruvaḻiyilum nilimoḻi yakaraṅkeṭutalum koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
(vēṟṟumiyil iyalpātal)
vāy+pukuvatu=vāypukuvatu
iṅku, vēṟṟumiyil iyalpāyiṟṟu.
(alvaḻiyil uṟaḻtal)
pāḻ+kiṇaṟu=pāḻkkiṇaṟu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu
iṅka alvaḻip paṇputtokiyuḷ uṟaḻntu vantuḷḷatu.
(alvaḻiyil-yakaram keṭutal)
kāy+yātu=kāyātu
iṅka, alvaḻiyil nilimoḻi iṟutiyakaram keṭṭatu.
(vēṟṟumiyil-yakaram keṭutal)
nōy+yāṉi=nōyāṉi
iṅku, vēṟṟumiyil nilimoḻi iṟuti yakaram keṭṭatu.
ilakkaṇa viti: yakara, rakara, ḻakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ varum ka, ca, ta, pakkaḷ variṉ alvaḻiyil iyalpāyum mikkum puṇarum. vēṟṟumiyil mikkum, valliṉattiṟku iṉamāṉa melliṉam uṟaḻntum puṇarum.
yaraḻa muṉṉark kacatapa valvaḻi
iyalpu mikalu mākum vēṟṟumi
mikalu miṉattō ṭuṟaḻtalum vitimēl
(na-nūṟpā 224.)
3. lakara, ḷakara īṟṟuppuṇarcci
(vēṟṟumi)
kal+kuṟi=kaṟkuṟi
muḷ+kuṟi=muṭkuṟi
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē lakara, ḷakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ valliṉam variṉ, lakaram ṟakaramākavum, ḷakaram ṭakaramākavum tiriyum.
(alvaḻi)
kal+kuṟitu=kalkuṟitu, kuṟkuṟitu
muḷ+kuṟitu=muḷkuṟitu, muṭkuṟitu
ivvāṟu, alvaḻiyilē lakara, ḷakara meykaḷiṉmuṉ valliṉam variṉ, orukāl iyalpākiyum orukāl tirintum varum.
(vēṟṟumi)
kal+māṭci=kaṉmāṭci
muḷ+māṭci=muṇmāṭci
ivvāṟu vēṟṟumiyilē lakara,ḷakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ melliṉam variṉ, lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tiriyum.
(alvaḻi)
kal+māṇṭatu=kaṇmāṇṭatu
muḷ+māṇṭatu= muṇmāṇṭatu
ivvāṟu avvaḻiyil lakara,ḷakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ melliṉam variṉ, lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tiriyum.
(vēṟṟumi)
kal+yāppu=kalyāppu
muḷ+yāppu=muḷyāppu
ivvāṟu, vēṟṟumiyilē lakara, ḷakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ iṭiyiṉam variṉ iyalpākum.
(alvaḻi)
kal+yātu=kalyātu
muḷ+yātu= muḷyātu
ivvāṟu avvaḻiyil lakara,ḷakara meykaḷiṉ muṉ iṭi yiṉam variṉ iyalpākum.
kal+tītu=kaṟṟītu
muḷ+tītu=muṭṭītu
iv veṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷil, tammuṭaṉ mayaṅkāta takaram vantapoḻutu, lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ iyalpākāmal muṟiyē ṟakara mākavum, ṭakaramākavum tirintuḷḷaṉa. alvaḻiyuḷ iṅṅaṉam varum tiripu oṉṟi maṭṭum ēṟpuḻikkōṭalāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
ilakkaṇa viti: lakara, ḷakaramākiya iraṇṭu meyyīṟukaḷum vēṟṟumiyil valliṉam vantāl, muṟiyē ṟakaramākavum, ṭakaramākavum tiriyum. alvaḻiyil valliṉam vantāl iyalpāyum tirintum varum. vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilum melliṉam vantāl, lakara, ḷakara īṟukaḷ muṟiyē ṉakaramākavum, ṇakaramākavum tiriyum. vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilum iṭiyiṉam vantāl, lakara, ḷakara īṟukaḷ iyalpākum.
laḷavēṟ ṟumiyiṟ ṟaṭavu malvaḻi
avaṟṟō ṭuṟaḻvum valivari ṉāmeli
mēvi ṉaṇavu miṭivari ṉiyalpum
āka miruvaḻi yāṉu meṉpa.
(na-nūṟpā 227.)
payiṟci viṉākkaḷ
1.	pala, cila eṉṉum ivviru coṟkaḷum tammuṉ tāmvariṉ
	evvāṟu puṇarum?
2.	pala, cila eṉṉum ivviru coṟkaḷum tammuṉ piṟavariṉ
	evvāṟu puṇavaruma?
3.	pū eṉṉum peyar valliṉam vara eṅṅaṉam puṇarum? 
	cāṉṟu taruka.
4.	teṅku eṉpataṉ muṉ kāy eṉṟa col variṉ evvāṟu
	puṇarum?
5.	marap peyarc coṟkaḷil cila, valliṉam varum, poḻutu
	vēṟṟumiyil evvāṟu puṇarum? cāṉṟu taruka.
6.	tēṉ eṉṉum colliṉmuṉ mūviṉa meykaḷum, vantu
	puṇarum muṟiyi viḷakkuka.
7.	piritteḻutuka: paṟpala, cilvaḷi, pūṅkoṭi, tēṅkāy.
8.	cērtteḻutuka:
kaḷa+kaṉi, mā+taḷir, tēk+kuḻampu, tē+malar
9.	ceyyuḷ vikāram ettaṉi vakippaṭum? avi yāvi?
	eviyēṉum iraṇṭiṟkuc cāṉṟu taruka.
10.	ṇakara,ṉakara īṟukaḷ vēṟṟumiyil valliṉam vara eṅṅaṉam
	puṇarum?
11.	ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ vēṟṟumiyil melliṉamum
	iṭiyiṉamum vara eṅṅaṉam puṇarum?
12.	ṇakara, ṉakara īṟukaḷ alvaḻiyil mūviṉamum vara eṅṅaṉam
	puṇarum?
13.	tūṇaṉṟu - puṇarcci viti kūṟuka.
14.	ya ra ḻa īṟṟiṉmuṉ valliṉam variṉ alvaḻiyil eṅṅaṉam
	puṇarum? vēṟṟumiyil eṅṅaṉam puṇarum? ovvor
	cāṉṟu taruka.
15.	lakara, ḷakara īṟukaḷ valliṉam variṉ alvaḻiyil
	vēṟṟumiyil evvāṟu puṇarum? cāṉṟu taruka.
16.	lakara, ḷakara īṟukaḷ melliṉam variṉ iruvaḻiyilum
	eṅṅaṉam puṇarum? cāṉṟu taruka.
17.	lakara, ḷakara īṟukaḷ iṭiyiṉam variṉ iruvariyilum
	eṅṅaṉam puṇarum?

5. poruḷ
1. akattiṇi
akattiṇi, (akam+tiṇi) akattē nikaḻum oḻukkam paṟṟiyatu. akapporuḷ eṉavumpaṭum. aḵtāvatu, otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum, tām tuytta iṉpattip puṟattārkka ittaṉmiyatu eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟa iyalātatāyt tam uḷḷattāl uyttuṇarum taṉmiya tākum. eṉavē, akattiṇi eṉap paṭṭatu.
akattiṇi vaki:
kuṟiñcittiṇi, mullittiṇi, marutattiṇi, ney taṟṟiṇi, pālittiṇi eṉa akattiṇi aintu vakippaṭum.
ivaṟṟuṭaṉ, kikkiḷittiṇi, peruntiṇi eṉṟa iraṇṭiyum kūṭṭi ēḻu vakiyākavum kūṟuvar.
aintiṇikkuriya poruḷ:
mēṟcollappaṭṭa aintiṇikaḷum mutaṟporuḷ - karupporuḷ - uripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟu vakiyāl vaḻaṅkappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ eṉṟa iraṇṭiyum paṟṟik kāṇpōm.
mutaṟporuḷ
nilamum, poḻutum mutaṟporuḷ eṉappaṭum.
1. nilam
kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉa nilam aintu vakippaṭum.
1.kuṟiñci-maliyum malicārnta iṭamum.
2. mulli-kāṭum kāṭucārnta iṭamum.
3. marutam-vayalum vayalcārnta iṭamum.
4. neytal-kaṭalum kaṭal cārnta iṭamum.
5. pāli-taṉiyāka nilam illi. kuṟiñci nilamum,		mulli nilamuma tattamakkuriya taṉmi tirinta pakuti pāli nilam eṉappaṭum.
2. poḻutu
perumpoḻutu, ciṟupoḻutu eṉap poḻutu iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.
perumpoḻutu
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir (kuḷir) muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉap perumpoḻutu āṟu vakippaṭum.
1. cittiri, vikāci-iḷavēṉiṟ kālam.
2. āṉi, āṭi-mutuvēṉiṟ kālam.
3. āvaṇi, puraṭṭāci-kār kālam.
4. aippaci, kārttiki - kūtir (kuḷir ) kālam.
5. mārkaḻi, ti-muṉpaṉik kālam
6. māci, paṅkuṉi-piṉpaṉik kālam
ciṟupoḻutu
kāli, naṇpakal, ēṟpāṭu, māli, yāmam, vikaṟi eṉac ciṟupoḻutu āṟu vakippaṭum.
1. cūriyaṉ utittatumutal pattu nāḻiki vari (kāli 6 maṇi mutal 10 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram kāli eṉappaṭum.
2. pakal pattu nāḻiki mutal irupatu nāḻikivari (10 maṇi mutal 2 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram naṇpakal eṉappaṭum.
3. pakal irupatu nāḻiki mutal muppatu nāḻiki vari (2 maṇimutal 6 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram ēṟpāṭu eṉappaṭum. (ēṟpāṭu=el+pāṭu. el=cūriyaṉ. pāṭu-maṟital.)
4. cūriyaṉ maṟintatu mutal pattu nāḻiki vari (māli 6 maṇi mutal 10 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram māli eṉappaṭum.
5. iravu pattu nāḻikimutal irupatu nāḻiki vari (iravu 10 maṇi mutal 2 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram yāmam eṉappaṭum. (yāmam=naḷḷiravu.)
6. iravu irupatu nāḻikimutal cūriyaṉ utikkumvari (iravu 2 maṇi mutal kāli 6 maṇi vari) uḷḷa nēram vikaḷi eṉappaṭum. (vikaṟi = viṭiyaṟkālam.)
aintu nilaṅkaḷukku uriya
perumpoḻutum - ciṟu poḻutum

1. kuṟiñci
kuḷirkālam, muṉpaṉikkālam eṉṟa perum poḻutukaḷum, yāmam eṉṟa ciṟupoḻutum kuṟiñci nilattuk kuriyaṉa.
2. mulli
kār kālamākiya perumpoḻutum, māliyākiya ciṟu poḻutum mulli nilattuk kuriyaṉa.
3. marutam
āṟu perumpoḻutukaḷum, vikaṟiyākiya ciṟupoḻu tum maruta nilattuk kuriyaṉa.
4. neytal
āṟu perumpoḻutukaḷum, ēṟpāṭu eṉappaṭum ciṟu poḻutum neytal nilattuk kuriyaṉa.
5. pāli
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, piṉpaṉi eṉṟa perumpoḻutu kaḷum, naṇpakalākiya ciṟupoḻutum pāli nilattuk kuriyaṉa.
karupporuḷ
ovvoru tiṇikkum uriya iyaṅkutiṇiyum, nilat tiṇiyumākiya poruḷkaḷ, karupporuḷkaḷ eṉap paṭum. avi, teyvam, uyarntōr, tāḻntōr, paṟavi, vilaṅku, ūr, nīr, pū, maram, uṇavu, paṟi, yāḻ, paṇ, toḻil eṉap patiṉāṉku vakippaṭum. avi varumāṟu.
kuṟiñcittiṇik karupporuḷ
1.	teyvam	-	murukaṉ
2.	uyarntōr	-	poruppaṉ, veṟpaṉ, 						cilampaṉ, kuṟatti,koṭic ci.
3.	tāḻntōr	-	kuṟavar, kāṉavar, kuṟattiyar.
4.	paṟavi	-	kiḷi, mayil
5.	vilaṅku	- 	puli, karaṭi, yāṉi, ciṅkam.
6.	ūr		-	ciṟukuṭi
7.	nīr		-	aruvinīr, cuṉi nīr
8.	pū		-	vēṅkip pū, kuṟiñcip pū, 					kāntaṭ pū.
9.	maram	-	cantaṉam, tēkka, akil,						acōku, nākam, mūṅkil.
10.	uṇavu	-	mali nel, mūṅkilarici,						tiṉi.
11.	paṟi	-	toṇṭakappaṟi.
12.	yāḻ	-	kuṟiñciyāḻ.
13.	paṇ		-	kuṟiñcippaṇ,
14.	toḻil	-	veṟiyāṭal, malinel						vitittal, tiṉikāttal, 					tēṉ eṭuttal, kiḻaṅkakaḻ 					tal, aruviyāṭal, cuṉi 					yāṭal.

				tōṉṟal, maṉivi, kiḻatti

				āyar, āycciyar.

5.	vilaṅku	-	māṉ, muyal
6.	ūr		-	pāṭi.
7.	nīr		-	kuṟuñcuṉinīr, kāṉyāṟṟu 					nīr.
8.	pū		-	kullippū, mullippū,						tōṉṟip pū, piṭavam pū.
9.	maram	-	koṉṟi, kāyā, kuruntam.
10.	uṇavu	-	varaku, cāmi, mutiri.
11.	paṟi	-	ēṟukōṭpaṟi.
12.	yāḻ	-	mulliyāḻ.

14.	toḻil	-	cāmi vitittal, varaku						vitittal, kaḷikaṭṭal,					arital, kaṭāviṭal, kol 					lēṟu taḻuvutal, kuḻal 						ūtal, kuravik kūttāṭal,					kāṉyāṟāṭal, kālnaṭi 					kaḷi mēyttal.
marutattiṇik karupporuḷ
1.	teyvam	-	intiraṉ
2.	uyarntōr	-	ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ, kiḻatti,
				maṉivi.
3.	tāḻntōr	-	 uḻavar, uḻattiyar, kaṭi 					yar, kaṭicciyar.
4.	paṟavi	-	vaṇṭāṉam, makaṉṟil, nā 					ri, aṉṉam, perunāri, 					kampuḷ, kuruku, tārā.
5.	vilaṅku	-	erumi, nīrnāy.
6.	ūr		-	pērūr, mūtūr.
7.	nīr		-	yāṟṟunīr, kiṇaṟṟu nīr, 						kuḷattunīr
